- **The void joins the tamper-evident chain** (`src/app/api/stock-takes/[id]/cancel/route.ts`): a cancelled sheet now writes a best-effort chained-HMAC audit event — action `VOID`, resourceType `STOCK_TAKE`, the zero law spoken in the event's own body (`stockMoved: 0, movements: 0`), the canceller's reason kept verbatim (ISO 27001 A.12.4 wants the event log to speak the exception; now it does). Best-effort by law: the chain must never block the void (the shift-closeout pattern).
- **The line finder** (`src/components/org/sheet-drill.tsx`): a big sheet's lines can outrun the fold — the drill grows a client-side needle (name or SKU, case-insensitive) over the lines it ALREADY holds. Zero new fetch; an honest "N of M" count; an empty face that names the needle and the sheet's truth; the summary tiles stay whole-sheet (the finder narrows the reading, never the arithmetic); the map keys the drill per sheet, so a fresh door opens with an empty needle by construction.

### Styled
- The violet family carries the hand — distinct from the amber split and the emerald success, 3:1 contrast on every chip; the move row rides a dashed hairline (a boundary, not a new card); the reset link is a dotted underline that solidifies on hover; the finder wears the house focus ring with the search glass inside the field; tabular-nums on every figure; dark halves from birth; the muted family (slate-500 / dark:slate-400) on every sub-line.

## [2.222.0] The sheet drill

The variance map's rows become doors. v2.220.0 gave the org its map — every branch's live counts with their progress, the recent roster with its variance chips — but the rows only SPOKE; they could not open. This round an org user can drill into any count sheet's lines from the console, and the restock draft gains its honest widening.

### Added
- **The org sheet drill** (`GET /api/org/stock-takes/[id]` + `src/components/org/sheet-drill.tsx`): a roster row (or a live row) opens ONE count sheet's lines org-side — the store-scoped detail route's 403 wall for org roles made the map all voice and no door. ORG_ROLES only (the kpi-summary contract), the read runs WITHOUT tenant, and every status drills: a COUNTING sheet shows its progress lines (the drill re-reads every 10s while the floor speaks), a CLOSED sheet its variance chips and the stored close-day cost, a CANCELLED sheet its void. READ-ONLY BY LAW — no count inputs, no close, no cancel; the branch sheet keeps the only pens.
- **The cost law rides the drill**: variance VALUE (the summary tile and the per-line cost) arrives null past `inventory.view.cost` — the face renders the honest absence ("the shillings stay behind the cost gate"), never a hidden zero.
- **The near-reorder band** (`restock-dialog.tsx`): the full scope's honest widening — the wire's `includeNear` (level × 1.5) joins the items APPROACHING the level. Defaults OFF (a restock drafts the shortage, not the comfort); the toggle discloses both states and the widened rows wear a "near band" chip so the draft reads which lines are below the level and which are approaching it. Split-aware: the widened list groups exactly as the tight one did.

### Styled
- The roster rows grew a chevron and a hover ground — the affordance says door before the click does; the drill wears the house org family (slate card, dark halves from birth, the muted family on every sub-line), the map's own chip ladder (amber COUNTING / emerald CLOSED / slate CANCELLED, red-700-short / emerald-700-over), tabular-nums on every figure, and the read-only footer that names the law.

### Measured
- `src/tests/sheet-drill-v2222.test.ts` (source pins: the org gate, the cost law, the read-only law, the drill faces, the near band's default-off and its disclosure) + `scripts/verify-v2-222-0.mjs` wired as `verify:v2-222-0`.

## [2.221.0] The supplier split

The restock draft learns that a hardware yard orders from MORE than one counter. v2.217.0/v2.218.0's dialog drafts every line to ONE supplier — the ledger's most-common hint — so a full-branch list whose rows point at two counters shipped all of them to the louder one. This round lets the draft split: one PO per supplier, exactly where each line's ledger hint points.

### Added
- **The supplier split** (`restock-dialog.tsx`): when the draft's shipping rows carry hints naming MORE than one active supplier, a toggle appears — "One PO" (the v2.217.0 behavior, untouched, the honest default) against "Per supplier (N)". In split mode each line drafts to its OWN hint's supplier; the lines the ledger never hinted follow the draft's effective supplier (the explicit choice wins for them — the hint-less rows need a name, and the note says so). Every group's PO notes name the split: "supplier split 2 of 3 (Nairobi Steel Traders)" — the ledger remembers how the draft was cut.
- **The per-group draft lines** — the list regroups under slim supplier dividers (name, line count, the group's shipping subtotal) so a split draft READS its cut while it edits; the per-group estimate rows join the estimate block (supplier + KES subtotal each) beneath the unchanged total and its indicative strip (the strip annotates the WHOLE draft's money figure, never a slice).
- **The multi-PO raise** — the submit walks the groups SEQUENTIALLY against the same `/api/purchase-orders` wire (one POST per supplier — a PO is a one-supplier document; the bridge composes the existing flow N times, it never invents a batch endpoint). Partial-failure honesty: a refused group never blocks the rest — the success face lists every raised PO (number, supplier, lines, the server's own total) AND names every refused group with the server's error, so a mid-run 429 is a named event, never a silent loss and never a fake success.
- **The split success face** — one PO keeps the v2.217.0 face exactly; several raise the roster: each row speaks its number, supplier and total, the run's server-truth grand total closes it, and "Draft another" clears the edits (the seeds re-derive; the toggle keeps its position — the next draft starts where the yard's habit points).

### Styled
- The split toggle wears the dialog's aria-pressed family (amber-700 active, the honest ring when at rest); the group dividers speak the amber-50/amber-700 chip family over the muted sub-lines (slate-500/dark:slate-400), tabular-nums on every subtotal, dark halves from birth; the refused rows wear the house red-700/50 family — the 3:1 law holds on every new surface.

### Measured
- `src/tests/restock-split-v2221.test.ts` (source pins: the group derivation, the explicit-choice fallback law, the sequential per-group notes, the partial-failure face, the toggle's honest availability, the strip's whole-draft figure) + `scripts/verify-v2-221-0.mjs` wired as `verify:v2-221-0`.

## [2.220.0] The variance map

Phase 2 of the stocktake arc: the count sheets reach the organization console. v2.219.0 gave the branch its sheet; this round gives the org its map — every branch's live counts with their progress, the recent roster with its variance chips, the rolling 30-day ledger impact as a money-voiced figure — and the sheet gains its honest way out.

### Added
- **The org block** (`stockTakes` in `/api/org/kpi-summary`, additive): live COUNTING sheets org-wide (with per-sheet counted progress), the 12 most recent sheets of any status (with per-sheet variance units and, past the cost gate, their stored close-day cost), and `window30d` — the rolling-30-day ledger impact read across EVERY closed sheet in the window (not just the named roster). The cost law rides the org wire too: variance UNITS speak to every org role, the SHILLINGS arrive only past `inventory.view.cost` (null otherwise — the face renders the honest absence).
- **The variance map** (`src/components/org/variance-map.tsx`, mounted on the console under the KPI row): the org face of the cycle count — four window tiles (counting now / closed 30d / net variance units / ledger impact), the live counts with amber progress bars, the recent roster with exact/over/short chips and the closed-by memory, and the honest step-asides (an older cached answer speaks `variance-map-stale`; an org with no sheets speaks the empty face). Zero new fetches — the map composes the SAME payload the KPI row already carries.
- **The cancel path** (`POST /api/stock-takes/[id]/cancel`): only a COUNTING sheet cancels (409 names the dead status); the COUNTING→CANCELLED claim is atomic inside the transaction (the double-click law the close speaks); NO stock moves, NO adjustments, NO movements — a cancelled sheet is a void; the optional reason (≤300 chars) is appended to the sheet's notes so the roster remembers WHY; the canceller becomes the closer-of-record.
- **The population picker** (branch panel create flow + `categoryId` on the create route): the sheet's scope speaks before it freezes — "Every active product" (the honest default) or "One category", picked from the categories wire's `includeProductCount` face so the confirm button quotes the REAL row count, resolved server-side against the store's own active products (a cross-store category matches nothing: the same honest empty-population refusal).

### Styled
- The map wears the house org family: slate card with dark halves from birth, the muted family on every sub-line, amber live progress against emerald closed chips, red-700-short / emerald-700-over variance chips (the 3:1 law), tabular-nums on every figure, and the cancel zone's two-step arm (red-700 confirm, "keep counting" disarm) so the destructive turn owes an intent.
- **The indicative strip reaches its FIFTH consumer** (`org-variance-indicative-strip`): the 30-day ledger impact rides the same zero-fetch observer the debt (v2.214.0), revenue (v2.215.0), desk (v2.216.0) and restock (v2.217.0) strips use — same silence law (KES pivot → silent; missing rate → silent; zero ledger impact → silent — a zero converts to a zero), same three-part voice, the tail naming what the figure is.

### Measured
- `src/tests/stocktake-v2220.test.ts` (source pins: the org block's cost gate, the cancel route's laws, the map's strip + step-asides, the picker's server-side resolution, the release face) + `scripts/verify-v2-220-0.mjs` wired as `verify:v2-220-0`.

## [2.219.0] The stocktake

The biggest confirmed feature gap gets its arc's first half. Every worklog since the bridge has named it: no model, no API, no UI for the cycle count — the floor counts on paper and the books guess. This round ships the count sheet end to end: the snapshot freezes, the floor counts against it, and the close speaks the variance into the ledger with the same ADJUSTMENT voice the manual adjustment flow has always used.

### Added
- **The models** (`StockTake`, `StockTakeLine` — schema + postgres migration `20261027230000_add_stock_takes`): a sheet belongs to ONE store, freezes its lines' `snapshotQty` inside a single transaction (one coherent moment — a sheet whose lines read different moments would count against a moving number), and carries the floor's `countedQty`/`variance` per line. Statuses follow the PO convention (free-text uppercase strings — Prisma enums are unsupported on SQLite and the provider flip is house law).
- **The wire** (`/api/stock-takes` list + create, `/[id]` detail, `/[id]/count`, `/[id]/close`, all `requireStoreAccess`-scoped): the ONE-OPEN-SHEET LAW (a store with a COUNTING sheet refuses a second — 409 with the live sheet's id); the zero law on counts (0 is a count, negatives are a lie); the close laws (EVERY line counted or 409 naming the holes; the COUNTING→CLOSED claim atomic inside the transaction so a double-click close cannot double-apply; each non-zero-variance line writes ONE ADJUSTMENT StockMovement with the session's actor and applies the counted − snapshot DELTA to current stock — sales during the count are preserved; `varianceCost` = variance × cost price ON THE CLOSE DAY, stored per line).
- **The branch UI** (`src/components/inventory/stock-take-panel.tsx`, mounted on the inventory tab): the list face (status chips, progress, resume/view), the create flow (optional notes — "open the sheet — freeze every active product"), the sheet face (progress bar, per-line count inputs with the snapshot as placeholder, variance chips exact/over/short, re-countable until close), the close gate ("Close refused — N lines still uncounted"), and the success face reading the SERVER's own numbers.
- **The cost gate**: variance VALUE (KES) speaks only to roles holding `inventory.view.cost` (the same gate the products API applies to costPrice) — a cashier counts units; the shillings stay off the wire for eyes without the permission. Units are units — every role hears the floor's arithmetic.

### Styled
- The panel wears the house inventory family: amber accents on light, dark halves from birth (`dark:bg-slate-900` panels), the muted family (`slate-500` / `dark:slate-400`) on every sub-line, emerald-700 close / red-700-short / emerald-700-over chips (the 3:1 contrast law), tabular-nums on every figure, the progress bar amber while counting and emerald complete.
- Count entry is edits-only state (the v2.218 derivation law's sibling): the input holds the floor's typing, a recorded count lands on the wire, the sheet refetches — no seed state to churn.

### Measured
- `src/tests/stocktake-v2219.test.ts` (source pins: the models' shape, the one-open-sheet law, the snapshot-in-transaction, the count zero law, the close laws — holes, atomic claim, delta arithmetic, ADJUSTMENT movements, session actor, close-day pricing, the cost gate, the UI's honest states) + `scripts/verify-v2-219-0.mjs` wired as `verify:v2-219-0`.

## [2.218.0] The full restock

The bridge learns the whole branch. v2.217.0's reorder bridge drafted from the org answer's NAMED rows — the loudest twelve items org-wide, the wire's honest window. A branch whose shortage outruns the cap could only draft the rows the answer happened to carry. This round gives the dialog a scope: the named rows (zero fetch) or the branch's OWN low-stock wire (one fetch, uncapped) — the restock story now covers every item at or below its level, and the modal discloses the cap instead of leaving it implied.

### Added
- **The scope toggle in the restock dialog** (`restock-mode-named` / `restock-mode-full`, an aria-pressed pair): "Named watchlist (N)" composes this answer's rows with ZERO fetch (the v2.217.0 zero-fetch law, untouched); "Full branch list" performs the dialog's second disclosed fetch — the branch's own `/api/products/low-stock` wire (store-scoped, uncapped), mapped onto the same `RestockItem` shape so every law below is scope-agnostic by construction (the supplier hint's last-PAID cost keeps precedence over the book cost, the SAME suggested-reorder arithmetic rides both surfaces). Each open starts on the named rows: the payload's story leads, the fetch is always a deliberate act. Switching the list RESEEDS the draft, and the mode note says so.
- **The cap disclosure in the watchlist modal** (`lowstock-cap-note`): when the org shortage outruns the route's 12-row window, the meta now names the window AND the way out — "the answer names the 12 loudest of N — each branch's Draft PO can switch to the full branch list to cover the rest." The muted family carries it (the counter moves 61 → 62).
- **The reseed law, hardened**: the draft reseeds on OPEN, on SCOPE flip, and on genuine row change — keyed by a CONTENT signature (`id:suggestion:cost` per row), never by array identity. A parent re-render handing the dialog a fresh array of equal rows can no longer wipe the user's edits mid-session (the identity-churn lesson, found by reading the v2.217 wire's inline-filter prop before it could bite).
- **Full-mode honesty states** (`restock-full-meta`, `restock-full-loading`, `restock-full-error`, `restock-full-empty`): the wire's own summary (total at/below level · out of stock · below level), a loading voice while the fetch runs, an honest refusal if it fails (with the named way back), and the truthful empty face when the branch's wire names nothing.
- **The ledger-hint chip** (`restock-supplier-hint`): when the draft's supplier came from the LEDGER's hints rather than the user's hand, the dialog says so and names whose ledger spoke — "the ledger points to Bamburi Cement Ltd — the draft begins where the ledger points." An explicit choice silences it.
- **Scope-named PO notes**: the raised draft's notes say which list it drafted from — "(named watchlist)" or "(full branch restock list)" — so the branch workspace's Purchase orders tab reads the draft's provenance at a glance.

### Styled
- **Per-line coverage bars in the draft** (styling detail): every draft line draws its stock against its reorder level at reading size — the SAME hue ladder the watchlist chips speak (out red → deep orange → amber → at-level emerald), aria-hidden, the numbers carrying the meaning. A full-list draft reads its urgency while it edits.
- **The draft lines scroll at reading height** (`max-h-[42vh]`) so an uncapped full-list draft never outruns the dialog; "Draft another" now reseeds the ACTIVE scope's rows (a fresh draft, never the shipped one's leftovers).
- **The supplier tally rides the ACTIVE scope**: a full-list draft reads the whole branch's ledger hints (the most-common last supplier across EVERY low item), not just the named twelve's.

### Measured
- `src/tests/restock-full-v2218.test.ts` (source pins: the scope toggle + its honest notes, the ONE disclosed fetch, the wire mapping's cost precedence, the content-signature reseed law, the zero laws in both scopes, the strip riding the active rows, the count law holding on the modal, the cap disclosure, the version metadata) + `scripts/verify-v2-218-0.mjs` wired as `verify:v2-218-0`.
- The v2.217 bridge pins updated in place where the law evolved (the mode-aware notes, the signature-keyed reseed deps, the memoized items prop); the muted-family counter re-anchored 61 → 62; the console-changelog anchors re-anchored 108/109/110/141 → 109/110/111/142.

## [2.217.0] The reorder bridge

The watchlist learns to act. Since v2.197.0 the organization console's reorder watchlist has been a read-only story — it SPEAKS the shortage but cannot act on it; the only "Create PO" path lived on an orphaned store-level panel imported nowhere, and the Purchase orders tab's own low-stock helper required knowing the flow existed. This round bridges the two halves of the reorder life: the watchlist raises the draft, the branch workspace carries the flow.

### Added
- **Per-branch Draft PO doors in the watchlist modal** (`restock-open-<branch>`): the watchlist's by-branch face gains a compact amber door per branch — scoped to that branch's named rows, disabled honestly when the rows carry no store. The door opens the restock dialog, the bridge's far bank.
- **The restock dialog** (`src/components/org/restock-dialog.tsx`, testids `restock-*`): the branch's named items as draft lines — quantity pre-seeded from the payload's suggestion, unit cost pre-seeded at the last PAID cost (the most recent PO line's `unitCost`, falling back to the product's book cost server-side), steppers and direct entry with the zero law (a zero line ships nowhere, the submit refuses an empty draft), supplier select from the branch's active roster defaulting to the MOST COMMON last supplier among the lines, optional expected date, and the honest estimate block: VAT-EXCLUSIVE, labeled as such, because the server adds VAT at the org's rate when the PO is raised — the success face speaks the server's own numbers (`restock-po-number`, DRAFT status, subtotal/VAT/total).
- **The wire carries the bridge's arithmetic**: `/api/org/kpi-summary`'s named watchlist now rides `storeId` (the POST target — a purchase order is a ONE-store document), `costPrice`, `suggestedQty` (the store-level low-stock formula `ceil(1.5 × level − stock)` — one arithmetic on both surfaces), and the supplier hint (`lastUnitCost`, `lastSupplierId`, `lastSupplierName` from ONE bounded read over the ≤12 named items' most recent PO lines, first-hit-per-product in JS). The watchlist rows wear the suggestion in the open (`restock-suggest-<sku>` "order N" chips) and the branch rows name the supplier hints.
- **The indicative strip's FOURTH consumer**: the restock draft's estimate is money-voiced by construction, so it carries the strip on the SAME observer terms as the debt (v2.214), revenue (v2.215) and desk (v2.216) strips — pre-resolved lens passed down from the component's ONE observer (`restock-indicative-strip/-figure/-rate/-voice`), KES pivot → silent, silent wire → silent, the ledger stays in KES. The watchlist modal itself stays count-voiced BY LAW (units short are units, never shillings).

### Removed
- **The orphaned low-stock alert panel** (`src/components/inventory/low-stock-alert-panel.tsx`): imported nowhere since birth — a full watchlist UI with a "Create PO" callback that no parent ever wired. The bridge makes it truly dead weight; the deletion is the round's honest cleanup.

### Measured
- `src/tests/restock-bridge-v2217.test.ts` (28 source pins: the wire's new fields, the dialog's zero laws, the fourth consumer's guards, the count law holding on the modal) + `scripts/verify-v2-217-0.mjs` wired as `verify:v2-217-0` (39 checks).
- `scripts/contrast-math-restock-bridge-v2217.mjs` — the new voices on their REAL grounds (amber door white-on-amber-700, suggestion chips amber-800 on amber-100, the strip's slate family light and dark), 10/10 PASS.

## [2.216.0] The second shelf

The topology arc completes both org shells wide. v2.215.0 moved the rates cache's supply into the console shell; this round gives the org desk the same treatment and the strip's third consumer — the desk's slice modals were the last money-voiced org surface the family hadn't reached.

### Added
- **The org desk banner gains the display-currency switcher** (`org-desk-currency-switcher`): /org/dashboard is its own boot (the shells full-navigate), so without a shelf of its own the desk's strips would repeat the v2.214.0 architecture-silence the console shelf fixed. The trigger wears the banner's own light voices (this banner is `bg-background` — no dark overrides needed, unlike the console's dark banner) and sits as the Badge/Global Chat group's first sibling.
- **The desk slice modal's indicative strip — the third consumer** (`BranchSliceModal`): the SAME observer (a pure cache watcher, `enabled: false`, the queryFn exists only to say so — the zero-fetch law holds three surfaces wide), the SAME guards (KES pivot, missing or non-finite rate, unknown code → silent), the SAME visual family (slate strip, dark half from birth). The figure converts the tile's own total through the wire rate, and the aria tail names the metric's own noun so the honesty sentence stays exact: "this strip does not change what the revenue is."
- **The count law, made structural**: only the money-voiced metrics (revenue, debt — `unit === 'money'`) convert; the four count-voiced metrics (transactions, lowStock, overdueRentals, pendingMpesa) stay count-voiced BY LAW — a count converted to shillings would be nonsense. The gate rides FIRST in the guard chain, on the wire's own units, not adjacency.

### Measured
- `scripts/contrast-math-desk-strip-v2216.mjs` — the strip's voices on the desk modal's REAL grounds (white/70 over the WHITE body — a brighter ground than the kpi-row panels' slate-50; slate-800/60 over slate-900 dark), 6/6 PASS.
- `src/tests/desk-strip-v2216.test.ts` (20 source pins) + `scripts/verify-v2-216-0.mjs` wired as `verify:v2-216-0` (38 checks).

## [2.215.0] The strip reaches its shelf

The topology round. v2.214.0 shipped the indicative strip with an honest contingency: the react-query cache is per-boot, and the QA drive could not make the strip speak within a single console session. This round proves WHY on the real surfaces — the strip-bearing modals live on the org console, the cache's only supply lived in the POS workspace, and the shells full-navigate between each other by design — then fixes it by TOPOLOGY, not by a law amendment: the supply moves into the shell where the consumers live.

### Added
- **The console banner gains the display-currency switcher** (`console-currency-switcher`): one first-open fills the shared `ADMIN_CURRENCY_RATES_QUERY_KEY` cache in the SAME boot, and the debt ledger's indicative strip speaks in real flows — open the switcher once, then open a door. The trigger wears the banner's own dark voices (`border-white/15`, `text-slate-200`, hover `white/10`) as a sibling of the Stores/palette controls; the switcher gains a typed `triggerTestId` prop (no untyped `data-*` passthrough).
- **The revenue modal's indicative strip — the second consumer**: the SAME observer, the SAME guards (KES pivot → silent, missing/non-finite rate → silent, unknown code → silent), the SAME visual family and dark half from birth. The figure follows the ACTIVE LENS — gross `weekTotal`, or the net face's `netWeekTotal` with the same `??` guard the net grid uses — and the aria tail keeps the family sentence: "this strip does not change what the revenue is." The count-voiced surfaces (transactions) deliberately stay count-voiced: a count converted to shillings would be nonsense, so the sibling choice was made on the wire's units, not adjacency.
- **The zero-fetch law is untouched**: the row still contains no `fetch(` call — the strip still observes, the switcher's own first-open remains the sanctioned supply.

### Fixed
- **THE SPEAKING FIX (found live in the QA drive)**: `kpi-row.tsx` destructured `const { activeCurrency } = useCurrency()` — but the hook returns the field AS `currency` (`return { currency: activeCurrency, … }`). The binding was `undefined`, so both indicative strips' pivot guard compared `undefined === 'KES'` and the pair lookup searched `code === undefined`: **the strips were architecturally silent — cache full, USD active, still no figure.** The v2.214.0 E2E "silence verified live" had passed for the wrong reason, and the speaking state was never capturable; this round's drive (switcher open → cache filled, trigger hover-truth speaking, modal mute) exposed it. The rename at the destructuring site (`const { currency: activeCurrency }`) keeps every downstream line untouched; three new source pins + three verify checks bury the pattern.

### Changed
- **The EXPERTS chip sweep** (carried since v2.199.0): the audit tab's severity badges (ERROR `red-600`→`red-700`, WARN `amber-700`→`amber-800`), the credit-control bucket and OVERDUE badges (`red-600`→`red-700`), the mpesa status badge (`red-600`→`red-700`, `amber-700`→`amber-800`), and the `pctColor` helper (`red-600`→`red-700`). With the installed palette the pre-fix states measure ABOVE the bar on their real grounds (red-600 on white 4.76:1) — a CONSISTENCY re-voice, honestly recorded, the v2.199 Store-Matrix lesson repeating; the red-600-on-red-50 ghost (4.36:1) explains why the family fled red-600, and the armored voices buy 6.1–7.1:1 of headroom.
- **The QA daemon probes before spawning** (`scripts/ems-daemon-3200.cjs`): three health probes over ~4.5s, and a healthy listener is never raced again — the v2.214.0 EADDRINUSE ops note closes.

### Measured
- `scripts/contrast-math-experts-chips-v2215.mjs` — **17/17 PASS** (fixed voices 6.42/6.13/7.13/6.81; pre-fix states recorded above the bar at 4.76/4.55/5.05/4.82; the red-50 ghost under at 4.36).
- `src/tests/strip-shelf-v2215.test.ts` (21 source pins, three of them the speaking fix) + `scripts/verify-v2-215-0.mjs` wired as `verify:v2-215-0` (42 checks).

## [2.214.0] The currency wire's first consumer

The closing round for the v2.212 honest-scope note. Since v2.210.0 the rates wire could store non-KES pairs "until a consumer exists" — the debt ledger's indicative strip is that consumer.

### Added
- **The indicative strip** (debt ledger modal, below the total): when the active display currency is NOT KES **and** the wire answered with that pair, the total's approximate value in the active currency appears as a muted strip — `≈ {figure} · 1 KES = {rate} · {source}` — with the closing voice "indicative; the ledger stays in KES." The `aria-label` speaks the full honest sentence including "this strip does not change what the debt is."
- **One wire, three surfaces**: the strip reads `ADMIN_CURRENCY_RATES_QUERY_KEY` — the SAME cache the switcher and the Exchange Rates card share — lazily (`enabled: debtOpen`), never polled, with the switcher's now-exported `RatesWireBody` shape and `RATE_SOURCE_PHRASE` provenance map (the one-voice law: no privately re-worded copies).
- **The canonical law holds everywhere**: `debt-ledger-total` keeps its literal (`kes(k.debt.total)`), the strip is gated on the computed `indicative` (never a truthy total), and silence stays silence — KES pivot, missing wire answer, non-finite rate, or unknown currency code all render NOTHING.
