## [2.213.0] The security posture speaks

The visibility round for the security program. The v2.212.1 remediation round (PR #343) landed SEC-01..SEC-04 server-side — the single fail-closed password verifier, the fail-closed CRON_SECRET gate on all 11 cron routes, the SUPER_ADMIN-gated /api/health/env, and the sanitizer drop — but a SUPER_ADMIN with a browser had no way to SEE any of it. The System desk's new **Security posture** card reads one wire and speaks what the environment admits.

### Added
- **`GET /api/admin/security-posture`** (SUPER_ADMIN-gated, `runWithoutTenant`): booleans and counts ONLY — the count of accounts still on the retired `hashed_` password format (SEC-01; which IS the count of people the fail-closed verifier locks out until `scripts/migrate-legacy-passwords.mjs --apply` rehashes them), whether CRON_SECRET / JWT_SECRET / NEXTAUTH_SECRET are present (SEC-02 + boot secrets; the VALUES never leave the server — presence via `Boolean()`, never interpolation), the pinned `GATED_CRON_ROUTES = 11` constant, and whether env diagnostics are production-gated (SEC-03).
- **The Security posture card** (admin desk, below Exchange Rates): four rows, each with its own measured chip palette (emerald calm / red locked-out-or-fail-closed / amber dev-unset / slate dev-open — light and dark from birth, 13/13 measured pairs), a Re-read affordance on the same wire key, an error state that claims nothing rather than guess, and a footer that speaks the secret law: "booleans and counts only — the payload carries no secret material."
- **The honest posture voice**: unset-in-production CRON_SECRET speaks red AND says the 403 wall is "the fail-closed law, not an outage" — a security wall reported as an outage would train the owner to disarma it. The legacy-hash row speaks the fix by its own command name, dry-run first.

### Tests
- **`src/tests/security-posture-v2213.test.ts`** (22 pins): the route's gate/escape/prefix laws, the secret-interpolation ban, the pinned-count law (readdirSync counts the real cron routes and demands the constant match), the section's door law, dark-half law, 17 testids, the fix-naming voice, the footer's secret law, and the admin-desk mount order.
- **`scripts/verify-v2-213-0.mjs`** (21 checks) wired as `verify:v2-213-0`; **`scripts/contrast-math-security-posture-v2213.mjs`** measures the card's 13 light+dark voices on their real grounds (13/13 PASS, amber-700-on-amber-100 recorded honestly at the 4.51 knife-edge).


## [2.212.0] One wire, two surfaces, one truth — now live

The reactivity round. The currency switcher and the Exchange Rates card have read the SAME wire since v2.210.0 — but each into its own pocket: the switcher fetched once per mount into local state, so a rate the card wrote left the dropdown stale until a full remount. This release the two surfaces share one query cache, the switcher learns to speak when the wire answered, a curl-only write finds its surface, and — the headline nobody expected — **the switcher finally switches**.

### Fixed — THE MISSING SLICE (the switcher's birth defect)
- **`src/lib/stores.ts` AppState now carries `activeCurrency: CurrencyCode` + `setActiveCurrency`** — the fields `useCurrency()` has selected since the multi-currency feature landed (0cde857) but the store NEVER implemented. Every selection click in the product's history threw `TypeError: ... is not a function`, swallowed by the global error handler as a "Runtime Error" toast, and the POS stayed silently KES-locked: the trigger fell back to the KES meta, `format()` converted against an undefined target, and the switcher's one job — switching — never worked once. Found live: the v2.212 E2E's first selection click surfaced the error the tsc projection had been flagging as pre-existing import-graph noise for rounds (`Property 'activeCurrency' does not exist on type 'AppState'` — the noise was the bug's signature). The default is 'KES' (the honest canonical), the setter is the ONE write path `onSelect` always called, the choice rides `mbt_app_store`'s partialize (browsers whose persisted state predates the slice keep 'KES' via the shallow persist merge — no migration), and the two pre-existing type errors die with the slice.

### Added — THE SHARED CACHE (one wire, two surfaces, one truth — live)
- **`ADMIN_CURRENCY_RATES_QUERY_KEY`** (`src/components/currency-switcher.tsx`, imported by the card): the one-writer law applied to the cache contract itself — a single literal for the query key both surfaces read. The switcher's read rides `useQuery` (the SAME key the card uses) with **`enabled: opened`** — the lazy law preserved (nothing fetched until the first dropdown open) — **`refetchInterval: false`** (never on a timer) and the card's 30s staleTime (one cache, one clock). After the first open the switcher is a live observer: a rate the card writes or resets invalidates the shared key and the chips and figures re-render WITHOUT a remount. Verified live: TZS set from the card → the dropdown's row spoke "admin-set rate" + "1 KES = TSh 18" on the next open of the SAME mount; the freshness line advanced from the first read's timestamp to the refetch's.
- **The freshness voice**: the dropdown footer's new last line — "Wire read at 11:47 PM." — composed from the cache's own `dataUpdatedAt` (honest recency, not a pretend "live"), the full timestamp on hover, `tabular-nums` on the figures. Before the first answer the footer stays exactly the three sentences it has always had; silence stays silence.
- **The trigger's hover truth**: once the wire answered and the active currency is not the KES pivot, the trigger's tooltip speaks the row voice — "1 KES = TSh 18 · admin-set rate". Before the wire answers (or on the base) the trigger claims nothing.

### Added — TRACK A PAIR (the last curl-only write finds its surface)
- **"Track a pair"** on the Exchange Rates card (SUPER_ADMIN-gated, disabled while the wire is down): its own dialog with its own direction sentence ("How many USD one TZS equals — the pair direction the rates wire stores"), two selects filtered so a pair can never match itself ("A pair needs two different currencies — the base and the quote cannot match."), a positive-finite rate validated before the POST, and the preview storing the figure exactly as typed. The route's pair enum has allowed non-KES pivots all along — this is the UI that finally writes them. The honest scope is spoken in the dialog itself: the switcher reads KES pairs only; a tracked pair is stored and shown in the strip until a consumer exists.
- **The custom-pair strip grew hands**: each badge now speaks its provenance, its direction, and its freshness on hover ("Pair updated … · admin-set · 1 TZS = 1.18 USD") and carries its own Reset affordance feeding the SAME AlertDialog + DELETE route the table rows use.
- **The pair-aware un-write**: the reset confirm now speaks the truth each pair kind earns — a KES pivot falls back to its built-in constant (the original sentence, byte-exact); a CUSTOM pair "has no built-in constant to fall back to — the pair simply leaves the wire and the strip. The switcher never read it, so no display figure changes." The success toast is pair-aware too ("Rate reset — the pair reads its built-in figure again." / "Pair removed — the custom rate has left the wire."). A door that lies is still a lie even in a confirm dialog.

### Measured — the style law holds
- `scripts/contrast-math-wire-v2212.mjs` measures the release's new voices on their REAL grounds BEFORE the literals were pinned: the freshness line on the popover ground (6.00 light / 5.71 dark), the track dialog's muted helpers on the dialog ground (5.75/6.12), the dialog's selects (18.97/15.27), and the custom-strip badge + reset affordance on the bg-card/80-over-background ground (18.81/15.52 and 5.95/5.80) — **10/10 PASS**, light and dark.

### Tests
- **`src/tests/currency-switcher-wire-v2212.test.ts`** (39 tests): the shared-cache law (one exported key, the card's inline literal banned), the lazy-observer law (enabled-on-first-open, refetchInterval false, staleTime parity, the honest-absence map), the freshness + hover-truth voices, the track-pair laws (enum parity with the route's zod, the no-self-pair filters, the direction sentence, the close-on-success law, the own-toast voice), the custom-strip reset feeding the shared DELETE path, the pair-aware confirm + toast, and the missing-slice pins that keep the store honest. The v2.210 sweep's implementation pins moved with the machinery (same laws, new home: the lazy gate is `enabled: opened`, the silence lives in the map builder).

## [2.211.0] The wire's writer finds its surface

The loop-closing round. Since v2.210.0 the `/api/currency/rates` endpoint could PERSIST admin-set rates — but the only writer was curl. A SUPER_ADMIN with a browser had no way to set, inspect, or undo a rate. The admin desk's new **Exchange Rates** card completes the loop over the SAME wire the switcher reads (one GET, one truth).

### Added — THE EXCHANGE RATES CARD (the writer surface)
- **`src/components/admin/currency-rates-section.tsx`** on the Admin desk (after the configuration editor): a table of every tracked currency with the KES figure ("1 KES equals"), the inverse ("1 unit equals"), the rate's honest source chip (the SAME measured palette the switcher uses — emerald admin-set 5.09, blue API 6.26, muted built-in 5.95 on the card ground; dark 9.88/8.75/5.80, measured by `scripts/contrast-math-rates-card-v2211.mjs` BEFORE the literals were written), and the last-updated timestamp on hover. The KES pivot row takes no actions ("the pivot never moves").
- **The one-writer voice**: `composeRatesProvenanceVoice` (in `src/lib/report-voices.ts`, the house's one-writer law) condenses the SAME entries the table renders into one spoken sentence — "4 currencies tracked — 2 static, 2 manual." Counts come only from the wire; zero and non-finite groups speak nothing; singular/plural is honest; an unwired card says nothing is tracked yet.
- **Set rate**: per-row dialog that POSTs one (KES, quote) pair. The dialog speaks the pair DIRECTION the route stores (KES-per-quote-unit — the v2.210 E2E's "pair semantics"), shows the switcher's figure live (1 KES = the inverse), validates a positive finite number, and CLOSES on success while the toast speaks the outcome ("Rate saved — 1 TZS = 0.055 KES is now admin-set."). A confirm surface must never outlive its own click (the v2.209 lesson).
- **Reset to built-in**: per-row destructive AlertDialog for rows whose source is not STATIC — DELETEs the stored override so the pair falls back to its built-in constant. The dialog closes by its own action; the toast speaks the reset.
- **THE DELETE ENDPOINT** (`DELETE /api/currency/rates?base=&quote=`, SUPER_ADMIN-only): the same pair enum the POST accepts, validated by zod; a pair with no stored row answers the honest 404 ("No stored rate for KES→TZS — this pair already reads its built-in rate.") — never a pretend success; a missing/unknown pair answers 422 with a spoken error.

### The door law, restated for the new surface
- Mutations are SUPER_ADMIN-only SERVER-side (POST and DELETE both `requireAuth(..., { roles: ['SUPER_ADMIN'] })` — verified live: STORE_OWNER 403 on both, unauthenticated 401). The client renders the action column and buttons only for SUPER_ADMIN and SPEAKS the restriction where they would have been ("Exchange rate management is restricted to the system administrator.") for every other admin-role eye. Read-only surfaces stay for everyone.

### Honest states
- A failed wire is an error row with a retry — "the figures are unavailable, not zero"; the empty sentence belongs to a loaded-and-empty wire only. Custom pairs (base ≠ KES, which the switcher does not read) render muted with their honest scope spoken ("Custom pairs stored (the switcher reads KES pairs only):"). The footer states the accounting truth ("the accounting currency remains KES") and what resetting means.
- `src/tests/currency-rates-card-v2211.test.ts` (31 tests) pins the whole law on the source of record: the DELETE contract (SUPER_ADMIN guard, query-param pair, the honest 404, the delete shape, `runWithoutTenant`), the door (role gate, spoken restriction, action column behind `canManageRates`), the dialog laws (closes on success, positive-number validation, the pair direction spoken, the preview computed from the same input the POST sends), the honest states, the measured chip literals, the admin-tab placement, and the composer's zero law + singular/plural + unwired sentence.

### E2E-proven live (on the shipped tree, SUPER_ADMIN persona)
- The card's voice composed from the live wire; Set rate walked end to end (TZS 0.055 saved → toast → chip flipped admin-set → the switcher's own row spoke "admin-set rate" with "1 KES = TSh 18"); Reset walked end to end (AlertDialog → confirm → chip flipped built-in → the Reset button disappeared → the voice recomposed); Retry All re-queued the 6 demo seeds ("6 item(s) have been re-queued for retry."); Purge Old spoke the honest zero ("0 item(s) older than 7 day(s) have been purged." — the seeds are fresh); console zero app errors.

## [2.210.0] The currency wire finds its model

The round the phantom-model sweep was born — and caught a second founding bug of the same class v2.209.0 killed. The `/api/currency/rates` route has queried `db.currencyRate` since birth while the model existed in NO schema: the `as unknown as PrismaClient` cast in `src/lib/db.ts` hid the missing delegate from TypeScript, the GET's try/catch masked it behind the static list (every rate labeled STATIC forever), and POST was a guaranteed runtime 500. Two files (`currency-utils.ts`, the route header) even documented the model as the designed store — the contract was written, the table never was.

### Added — THE SWEEP LAW (the class-killer)
- **`scripts/phantom-model-sweep.mjs` + `src/tests/phantom-model-sweep-v2210.test.ts`**: every `db.<delegate>` access in a file that imports the Prisma client must name a model the schema declares — comments and string literals stripped before matching (prose like `@db.Decimal` or "db.ts" can never read as a delegate), client-side IndexedDB code out of scope by construction (it never imports `@/lib/db`). The test re-implements the sweep on the source of record, so CI enforces the law without the script. The sweep converged on exactly ONE real phantom: `db.currencyRate`.
- **The `CurrencyRate` model exists** (`prisma/schema.prisma` + migration `20261022040000_add_currency_rates`): (base, quote) pairs with the compound `@@unique` the route's `where: { base_quote }` always demanded (without it the upsert was unwritable), the Decimal rate, the `MANUAL | API` source, timestamps; `@@map("currency_rates")`; deliberately NOT store-scoped (global financial configuration, read inside `runWithoutTenant`).

### Fixed — THE WIRE STANDS ON REAL GROUND
- **POST /api/currency/rates works for the first time in the product's history** (E2E-proven: two rates upserted live, the upsert riding the new compound unique).
- **GET /api/currency/rates is finally authenticated**: the header documented "any authenticated user" since birth, but the handler shipped unauthenticated — `withErrorBoundary(requireAuth(getHandler))` now matches the documented contract (verified live: no-cookie GET 401, no-cookie POST 403).
- **The GET catch tells the truth about when it fires**: the old comment ("if the table doesn't exist yet") described a table that does now exist — the catch is outage-only, and the static fallback stays honest (every currency's `source` reads STATIC; the client can never mistake a built-in constant for an admin-managed rate).

### Added — THE SWITCHER SPEAKS EACH RATE'S SOURCE
- **`CurrencySwitcher` learns its consumer role** (the endpoint always needed one): the first open lazily fetches `/api/currency/rates` (never on mount, never on a timer, one attempt per mount), overlays DB rows on the static list, and each row speaks its provenance with a measured chip — emerald **admin-set** (MANUAL, 5.09:1), blue **API** (6.26:1), muted **built-in** (STATIC, 6.00:1) on the popover ground; dark variants 9.67/8.58/5.71 (measured by `scripts/contrast-math-switcher-chips-v2210.mjs` BEFORE the literals were written). The row's aria-label names the provenance too ("Switch display currency to US Dollar (USD) — admin-set rate").
- **A wire that never answers renders NO chip** — honest absence, never an invented source; the footer's "indicative" sentence stays the truth and now explains the chips ("Each chip names its source: admin-set, API, or built-in.").

### Style — THE EXPERT-TABLE CHIP AUDIT (the badge sweep's final third)
- **Eleven stragglers re-voiced on the white-card ground** (`src/components/org/experts.tsx`, measured by `scripts/contrast-math-expert-chips-v2210.mjs`): ten TEXT sites riding `red-500` (3.82) or `orange-500/600` (2.89/3.59) — the System tab's check-fail text, the env badge's fail branch, the trial-balance "Off" tile, the debt "Outstanding"/"90+ days" tiles, the rentals "Overdue" tile, the M-Pesa "Mismatch" tile, the health breakdown's failing scores, the low-stock tile and the reorder-watchlist figures — now `red-700` (6.42) / `orange-700` (5.23). The Unavailable panel's AlertTriangle icon was GRAPHICS below the 3:1 bar (`amber-500`, 2.05) — now `amber-600` (3.05). The honest passers stay untouched (red-600 badges 4.76, amber-700 5.05, emerald-700 5.37, slate-500 4.77).

**GATES**: vitest 4493/4493 (211 files; NEW phantom-model-sweep-v2210 22 tests: the sweep law + the stripping machinery + model/migration pins + route pins + switcher chips + the expert audit pins) · lint 0 errors / 532 warnings (exactly at baseline) · both contrast scripts green · build exit 0 (OOM-safe) · live E2E on :3200 @2.210.0: What's New heads 2.210.0, all four switcher rows speak their source (USD/UGX admin-set after the live POST, KES/TZS built-in), no-cookie GET 401 / POST 403, the expert Debt tab renders the re-voiced tiles, console zero app errors.

## [2.209.0] The dead-letter queue speaks its composition

The round the DLQ desk rendered what its wire always carried — and the round the live E2E caught the desk's founding bug. Since Phase 8 the `/api/health/dlq` response has included a `metrics` block with `byOperationType`, `byTargetService` and `oldestPendingAt` — the desk's queryFn CAST the shape and the surface rendered none of it (the only mention was the type assertion). Then the E2E went to seed the queue for the button-press walk and the desk's own wire answered 500: **the `DeadLetterQueue` model never existed in any schema**.

### Fixed — THE TABLE THAT NEVER WAS (the bug three layers of masking hid)
- **The `DeadLetterQueue` model exists** (`prisma/schema.prisma` + migration `20261022000000_add_dead_letter_queue`): the lib (`src/lib/dead-letter-queue.ts`) has written every query against `db.deadLetterQueue` since Phase 6, but no schema carried the model. The cast in `src/lib/db.ts` (`as unknown as PrismaClient`) hid the missing delegate from TypeScript, the build passed, and every DLQ-shaped route (`/api/health/dlq` GET+POST, `/api/health/dlq-demo`, the retention purge) was a runtime 500 since birth. The model carries the full contract the lib assumed (storeId/operationType/targetService/payload/status/retryCount/maxRetries/priority/sourceEntity*/lastError/nextRetryAt/**resolvedAt** — the retention purge's dateField/metadata/enqueuedAt/updatedAt) with the indexes its query shapes demand. NOT in `STORE_SCOPED_MODELS`: the ops desk reads across stores by design and self-scopes via an explicit, validated storeId param.
- **The aggregator's silent-zero fallback died**: `/api/health/system-status` caught the DLQ failure and fell back to ALL-ZERO metrics + 'warning' — the desk rendered "0 pending, 0 retrying" while the wire 500ed, and the system verdict read "Degraded" as the mask. Now a failed DLQ wire reports status 'error' + an additive `detail` ("counts are unavailable, not zero") — and the desk's DLQ quick-stat speaks it. With the table live, the verdict reads "All Systems Operational" again — this time honestly.
- **The DLQ tab's empty state no longer lies**: a failed `/api/health/dlq` query rendered "No DLQ items found — All external service calls are processing normally" over the 500. The loaded-and-empty path keeps that sentence; a query error now shows "The DLQ wire failed to answer — the queue's figures are unavailable, not zero" with a retry button (`isError` branch).
- **The desk's created-column NaN died**: the tab read `item.createdAt` but the lib's canonical stamp is `enqueuedAt` — every row would have rendered "NaNd ago". The route now aliases `createdAt` from `enqueuedAt` (same instant, the enqueue moment).
- **The row action buttons gained accessible names**: the retry/cancel icon buttons were tooltip-only — a screen reader met an unnamed button. `aria-label="Retry {type} item"` / `"Cancel {type} item"`.

### Fixed — THE DOORS THAT 403 (the honest door law, found by pressing the buttons)
- **The desk stopped rendering lie-buttons**: every mutation route (`/api/health/dlq` POST, `/api/health/circuit-breaker` POST) is SUPER_ADMIN-only server-side, but the desk rendered Retry/Cancel/Retry All/Purge and breaker Reset/Force Open/Force Close to STORE_OWNER too — doors that 403 on press. The desk now derives the same gate from the auth store (`canOperateQueue`), renders the buttons only where the server would answer, and SPEAKS the restriction where they would have been ("Queue operations are restricted to the system administrator" / breaker equivalents). Read-only surfaces stay for every admin-role eye.
- **The breaker row no longer eats its own dialog triggers**: the row's expand/collapse `onClick` bubbled from inside the expanded block — clicking Reset collapsed the row and unmounted its just-opened dialog before Radix could render it. The admin could never press the buttons at all. The toggle now ignores clicks from the row's interactive children (`closest('button, [role="dialog"], a')`).
- **Confirming a dialog closes it**: the destructive Confirm button now closes its dialog after firing — the action's outcome speaks through the toast ("Circuit breaker action completed (1 affected)"), not through a dialog that outlives its own click.

### Added — THE IN-FLIGHT COMPOSITION STRIP (the DLQ tab's deaf spot)
- **A proportional stacked bar by operation type**: segments divide by the in-flight total computed from the SAME filtered entries the bar draws — no invented proportions. `role="img"` + one aria from the new composer.
- **`composeDlqCompositionVoice`** (the one-writer law, `report-voices.ts`'s fifth composer): caller-ordered entries (the wire's own order — no re-ranking invented), the zero law (zero-count and non-finite entries speak nothing and are never summed), singular/plural honesty ("In flight: 1 item" / "4 items"), the service breakdown joined when the wire carries one, and the oldest age named only when the caller pre-formats one. An empty queue speaks "The in-flight queue is empty — nothing is waiting or retrying."
- **Operation chips** with colour-matched dots (the legend disambiguates when the palette cycles), **service chips** (the secondary breakdown, muted), and the **oldest-waited line** — a DURATION voice ("has waited 4m" via the new `formatWaitedAge`), distinct from `formatTimeAgo`'s point-in-time voice ("4m ago").
- **The scope is stated where the numbers are**: the header badge reads "pending + retrying only" and the caption says "completed and cancelled are history, not composition" — the composition counts the wire's own groupBy scope, never the queue's history.

### Style (mandatory)
- The bar's palette is the measured -600 family, numbers measured with the house WCAG machinery before the comment was written: blue-600 5.17, purple-600 5.38, teal-600 3.74, amber-600 3.19, emerald-600 3.77, cyan-600 3.68 — every stop clears the 3:1 graphics bar on the white card ground; segments and legend chips cycle the SAME constant (one truth per name). Counts ride `tabular-nums`; the zero state is a plain honest sentence, never a percentage of nothing.

### Tests
- NEW `src/tests/dlq-composition-v2209.test.ts` (28 tests): the composer's unit law (honest nothing ×3 shapes, singular/plural, caller order pinned against alphabetical drift, service-part presence, oldest-age presence, NaN-poison + Infinity + zero-count drops, the whole-sentence mixed queue); the surface pins (strip exists, scope badge, history caption, role="img" + composer wiring, the real-wire gate, the derivation's zero-law filter, segments dividing by the same filtered total, the duration-vs-timestamp voice split, the measured palette, the two-cycle pin); THE BUG PINS (the model + @@map in the schema, the migration's table + indexes, the model NOT in STORE_SCOPED_MODELS, the route's createdAt alias, the aggregator's no-invented-zeros law, the query-error-is-not-the-empty-state law, the aria-named row buttons); THE DOOR LAW (three SUPER_ADMIN guards read from source, the canOperateQueue gate, the three spoken restrictions, the bubble guard, the confirm-closes law); the wire + survival pins.
- Treadmill: the 37 suite-level pkg pins rode to 2.209.0; the four head-pin counters re-anchored 99/100/101/132 → 100/101/102/133; the seven "re-anchored to the new head" meta-pins followed to 100; whats-new heads the 2.209.0 entry.

### The live E2E that earned the round (owner + super-admin personas on :3200 @2.209.0)
- Owner: the composition reads "In flight: 8 items — SEND_EMAIL 2, SEND_SMS 6. By service: resend-email 2, twilio-sms 6. The oldest item has waited 11m." word-for-word; the restriction is spoken; no action buttons render; console zero errors.
- Super admin: Cancel pressed back (8 → 7 items live); the demo breaker driven OPEN via `/api/health/circuit-demo`, the Circuits tab reflects "0 open, 1 half-open"; Reset's dialog OPENS and SURVIVES (the bubble fix), Confirm closes it (the close fix), the toast speaks "Circuit breaker action completed (1 affected)", and the desk's own Refresh reads "0 open, 0 half-open" — the state machine walked by hand, end to end, on the shipped tree.

## [2.208.0] The system health desk finds its door

The round the app's largest orphan got its route. The Phase-8 System Health surface — 1,266 lines of live-wire monitoring (aggregated system status, circuit breakers with admin actions, a dead-letter queue with retry/purge, ISO compliance rings, retention + audit + access-control views) — had been COMPLETE for a hundred versions and unit-voiced in v2.207, but **no tab id, loader, sidebar row or switch case ever pointed at it**: `rg system-health-tab` matched only its own tests. This round routes it and finishes the voice + contrast pass its routing finally makes testable live.

### Added — THE ROUTE (five wiring points, all type-enforced)
- **`AppTab` gains `'system-health'`** (`src/lib/stores.ts`) — the union, `TAB_LOADERS`' `Record<AppTab, TabLoader>` and `TAB_CONFIG` now must agree at compile time.
- **`src/lib/tab-preload.ts`** carries the loader entry pointing at the real tab file (sidebar pointer-enter prefetch warms the chunk for free).
- **`TAB_CONFIG`** gains the door: `{ id: 'system-health', label: 'System Health', icon: HeartPulse, roles: ADMIN_ROLES }` — ADMIN_ROLES matches the privileged detail `/api/health/system-status` serves (SUPER_ADMIN + STORE_OWNER full view; the server re-validates per request and serves a summarised view to everyone else, and the retention/audit sections name their own SUPER_ADMIN requirement in the surface).
- **`NAV_GROUPS`** places it as the final item of Compliance & System — the admin cluster reads security → admin → the desk that watches them.
- **The shell switch** renders it behind `SectionErrorBoundary sectionName="System Health"` + `Suspense` (the lazy case takes no props, like security/admin).

### Added — THE SILENT PICTURES SPEAK (the routing makes them E2E-provable)
- **The database response-time bar speaks**: an honest aria composed from the wire — "Database response time Nms — within the 1,000ms caution line" (or "past" it). The voice names the WIRE's own warning threshold (the 1,000ms branch that flips the server verdict); the 2,000ms bar scale is a paint choice and is never called a threshold.
- **Both ISO score bars speak**: "ISO 27001 score N of 100 — strong/fair/at risk" — the verdict derives from the SCORE itself (>= 90 / >= 70, the same thresholds the quick-stat compliance card paints), never from the system's overall status (a different wire).
- **The refresh cadence is stated on the surface**: the header subtitle now says "auto-refreshes every 30s" — the `refetchInterval: 30000` the code always had, made visible.

### Fixed — THE DEAD WEIGHT (cut, not commented)
- **The hand-rolled `Loader2` SVG died**: a 17-line lucide clone at the file's bottom shadowed nothing and everything — the real `Loader2` is imported from `lucide-react` like every other surface.
- **The never-opens `ConfirmDialog` died**: a controlled dialog bound to `confirmAction` state that was only ever set to `null` (the real confirmations all use per-action wrappers with children). The state and the orphan block are gone; the mutation callbacks' toast + invalidation behaviour is untouched and pinned.
- **The dark-mode caption stragglers** (`dark:text-emerald-500` / `dark:text-amber-500`) joined the badge family's `dark:text-*-400`.

### Fixed — THE CONTRAST LAW HOLDS ON THE NEW DOOR (measured pairs, same WCAG machinery)
- The quick-stat icons rode emerald-500 (2.54) / amber-500 (2.15) → emerald-600 (3.30) / amber-600 (3.19); the DLQ stat-card icon map likewise (amber-500 → amber-600, blue-500 → blue-600, emerald-500 → emerald-600). red-500 (3.76), slate-500 (4.76) and the card-header blue-500 (3.68) already clear the 3:1 graphics bar and stay.
- The card-header accents re-voiced: teal-500 (2.49) → teal-600 (3.74), purple-500 (borderline) → purple-600, amber-500 (2.15) → amber-600 — including the failure-state icon.

### Tests
- NEW `src/tests/system-health-route-v2208.test.ts` (22 tests): the route exists end to end (union, loader→file, TAB_CONFIG row with ADMIN_ROLES + real icon, NAV_GROUPS placement, shell case with SectionErrorBoundary + Suspense, no duplicate ids/labels, the server wire's requireAuth guard); the silent pictures' aria compositions pinned (wire-derived caution-line voice, score-derived ISO verdicts, the stated cadence, the v2.207 ring voice kept); the measured pairs hold (light-ground -500 bans, the exact -600 maps, the v2.207 PulseIndicator dots); the dead weight stays dead (no hand-rolled Loader2, no confirmAction, the three live wires still read).
- `scripts/probe-graphics-v2207.mjs` still 63/63 — the probe's Scene 2 already watched this tab and every pin survived the round.
- The v2.109 nav order pin re-anchored with a round stamp (Compliance & System ends admin → system-health); the 37 suite-level pkg pins and the four whats-new head-pin counters re-anchored 98/99/100/131 → 99/100/101/132; whats-new heads the 2.208.0 entry.

## [2.207.0] The admin desk tells the truth; the quiet minis speak

The round the admin desk stopped lying and the last quiet pictures learned to speak. The v2.205 honesty pass swept the reports tab; this round caught the SAME disease living on the admin tab — four invented constants and a fake check history rendered as system health — plus the v2.204 graphics-bar audit's blind spot (the admin/system-health desks' -500 marks) and four silent pictures (two sparklines, one trend chart, two score rings).

### Fixed — THE HONESTY PASS (the admin desk's fabrications deleted)
- **The invented system metrics died**: `cpuUsage = 23`, `memoryUsed = 67`, `activeSessions = 3` and `dbSizeMB = 2.4` — four hardcoded constants rendered as real system health with progress bars, status colors and pulse dots — are DEAD. The browser has no CPU wire, so that gauge died with its fabrication and the card now speaks the health wire's actual verdict (Healthy / Degraded / Unhealthy + the wire's own response time). Memory is REAL heap-of-RSS math (two measured numbers, an honest "Measuring…" state while the wire answers). The DB-size card became the **App build** card: the version, database provider and app mode the wire actually runs. Active sessions read the wire's structured counts.
- **The fake check history died**: the API-latency sparkline booted with TWELVE invented response times rendered as "Avg over last 12 checks". The history now starts EMPTY; the caption speaks "Measuring — the first live check is in flight" until a real check lands, then "Avg Xms over the last N checks" — N counted from real checks only. A failed MANUAL refresh is recorded (999) exactly like the interval path — the sparkline never hides a failure it lived through.
- **The unconditional "Operational" badge died**: the header badge painted healthy green even while the health endpoint answered degraded. The verdict now rides `/api/health`'s real status, with an honest "Checking wire…" state before the first answer.
- **The client-side "uptime" timer died**: the card counted seconds since the browser tab mounted and called it uptime. It now speaks the server process's real `uptimeSeconds` ("Since last restart · server process").
- **THE HEALTH WIRE GREW STRUCTURED COUNTS (additive)**: `/api/health`'s `database_stats` check now carries `counts: { users, products, transactions, sessions }` alongside the unchanged human detail string — the panel reads structured fields instead of parsing prose, and the back-compat contract is pinned.

### Added — THE QUIET MINIS SPEAK (four silent pictures + two rings)
- **`src/lib/report-voices.ts` grew four composers** (the one-writer law holds): `composeLatencyVoice` (samples oldest-first, extremes named from the same samples, a single check never claims an extreme it cannot compare), `composeDailyRevenueVoice` (calendar order, the zero-day law — a day with nothing says "no sales", a range where nothing was ever sold names no loudest day), `composeTrendVoice` (every bucket with its real transaction count, totals once, the dashed previous-period line named ONLY when the wire carries one) and `composeScoreVoice` (the ring's fill IS the score; a missing score speaks the absence, never a percentage of nothing).
- **The admin latency sparkline speaks** (both call sites): every real check in order with slowest/fastest named.
- **The transactions tab's daily trend speaks**: every day in calendar order, quiet days honest, the loudest day named — figures pre-formatted through `formatKES` so the Ksh voice holds; a caption row rides under the chart ("N days on the chart · figures in Ksh").
- **The analytics sales trend speaks**: every bucket's revenue + real transaction count, the period totals once, the dashed line's meaning declared.
- **Both score rings speak**: the security gauge ("Security score 92 of 100 — Good") and the system-health compliance ring ("Overall compliance score N of 100 — healthy/degraded/unhealthy").

### Fixed — THE CONTRAST BLIND SPOT (the measured pairs, same WCAG machinery)
- **The admin health panel's marks re-voiced**: the good/warning bars and dots rode green-500 (2.28) / yellow-500 (1.92) — below the 3:1 graphics bar — now green-600 (3.30) / yellow-700 (4.92); the warning TEXT rode text-yellow-600 (3.3 — below the 4.5:1 text bar) now text-yellow-700; the latency card's spark rode text-orange-500 (2.15) now text-orange-600 (3.56). red-500 (3.76) passes and stays.
- **The system-health tab's marks re-voiced**: the status pulse dots (emerald-500 2.54 / amber-500 2.15 → emerald-600 3.77 / amber-600 3.19), the DB-latency bars, the ISO 27001/9001 compliance bars (emerald-500 → emerald-600, teal-500 2.49 → teal-600 3.74) and the -500 icon accents.
- **The latency bar caps at 100%** — a 999ms outlier used to render the track at 199.8% (an overflow, not a reading).
- **The single-point NaN guard**: both mini charts divided by `length − 1` and painted an invisible NaN line for a single sample; a dot on the grounding line speaks now.

### Style (mandatory)
- Every 9px caption in the admin tab grew to 10px; the latency/memory captions and the trend caption ride `tabular-nums`; the sparklines gained a grounding baseline (muted/40, non-essential) and a marker dot on the latest point; the dimmed `/70` sub-voices on the stat cards died for `text-muted-foreground` (the v2.205 quick-stat pattern).
- The transactions tab's `transactions` array is wrapped in its own `useMemo` — the conditional that re-created the array identity every render made every downstream memo's deps unstable (the whole `exhaustive-deps` warning class ended instead of growing).

### Tests
- NEW `src/tests/admin-truth-v2207.test.ts`: the fabrication bans (constants, seed array, fake badge, client uptime) pinned in reverse; the four composers unit-tested including the zero-day law, the NaN-poison drop and the single-sample no-extremes rule; the aria wiring per surface; the measured pairs verified against the WCAG math with the six buried -500 ghosts confirmed BELOW the bar in reverse; the additive counts contract.
- `scripts/probe-graphics-v2207.mjs` (63 checks) runs the same law against source with CODE-only scans (the round's own comments name the ghosts they buried — the v2.204 lesson).
- `verify:v2-207-0` wired — see `scripts/verify-v2-207-0.mjs`. whats-new heads the 2.207.0 entry.

## [2.206.0] The best-sellers stand up, honestly

The round the reports tab's last honest-empty section got its real wire — and the live E2E earned its keep three more times: one client-normalizer flattening (a wire field name that silently destroyed the payload shape), one imagined-wire contract (the Trends view rendered a shape the API never sent), and one rank-chip contrast failure hiding in plain sight since the list was born.

### Added — TOP PRODUCTS RIDES A REAL WIRE
- **The step-aside is history**: `reportsApi.getFastMovingReport` (new) fetches the purpose-built sold-quantity aggregation `GET /api/reports/fast-moving` (SaleItems → per-product quantity/revenue/saleCount over the picked range, date-range aware, limit 20, only when the tab shows top products). The chart, the rank list, the voice and the CSV export all speak the same real array — Bamburi Cement 100 sold / Ksh 75,000 on the seeded day, exactly what the API returns.
- **The honest totals strip**: units moved (300), products sold (23), revenue in range (Ksh 324,510.00) — straight off the wire's own summary, above the list, in the tab's chip voice with tabular-nums figures. Never a fabricated chip.
- **The voice carries quantities**: "Top products by revenue: Bamburi Cement 50kg (100 sold) Ksh 75,000.00 (37.2%), …" — composed from the same array; the Ksh voice kept (the v2.205 guard still bans a literal KES here).
- **NAMING AS CONTRACT**: the wire's array rides `fastMovers`, NOT `products` — the SPA's request() normalizer flattens an id-less envelope carrying a `products`/`items`/`data` array down to that bare array, silently dropping `summary` and `period` (the live E2E caught the UI showing the empty state against a 200 with full data). The route and the client type document the constraint; the guard test pins it.

### Fixed — THE TRENDS VIEW FINALLY SPOKE ITS OWN WIRE
- **The imagined contract died**: the tab rendered `row.current/previous/share/changePct` off a wire that carries `recentRevenue/previousRevenue/growthPct`, and read `trends.growing/declining` off a wire that names them `topGrowing/topDeclining` — every category row painted `Ksh 0.00 · NaN%` on LIVE data and the growing/declining sections rendered their empty states forever. The adapter now maps the wire's actual names (pinned by unit tests), computes the honest share (current / real total, 0 when the total is 0 — never NaN) and marks `changeKnown` false where the wire's growth is null.
- **"new" instead of a percentage of nothing**: a category or product with no previous period now speaks a "new" chip in the table, the drill-down and the aria ("new this period") — a fabricated +0% or NaN% is never rendered.
- **Real stock on the declining list**: the trend wire rows now carry `currentStock` (the same findMany, zero extra queries); the tooltip and voice speak "N units left" only when the wire knows, "on-hand stock unknown" when it doesn't — never an invented zero.
- **The growth bar plots measurable growth only**: new products stay out of the bars (a zero bar for a new gainer is a lie too) and the KPI card counts the wire's full gainer list with "· N new" as the subtitle; the empty state explains the baseline honestly when every gainer is new.
- **The Frequently Bought Together section spoke its first real words**: the tab's store-level "pairs" call was a GUARANTEED 400 (the route is product-scoped and demands `productId`/`productIds` — the old call sent neither, failed silently forever, and "Pairs Found" always read 0). The call is now SEEDED with the trends wire's own top gainers (enabled only once the seeds exist, keyed on them), the rows speak the co-bought item with its real co-occurrence count, category, price and on-hand stock, and the card subtitle names the movers the pairings are relative to. Honest empty states: "No tracked movers yet to seed the pairing" vs "No co-bought patterns in the recorded transactions yet".
- **The Trends section's silent 401 died**: the tab's two raw fetches (`/api/trends/analysis`, `/api/recommendations/frequently-bought`) rode `credentials: 'same-origin'` with NO Authorization header — on a token-only session (the SPA's own login shape) both endpoints answered "Authentication required" and the whole Trends & Predictions view sat on its empty states forever. Both now carry the session header like `request()` does (`sessionAuthHeaders()`); the guard test bans the header-less fetch shape.

### Style (mandatory)
- **The rank badges learned the 4.5:1 text bar**: the old trio (yellow-500 2.14 / gray-400 2.54 / amber-600 3.07 with white text — the rank NUMBER is text riding a fill) re-voiced gold/silver/bronze to yellow-700 (4.93) / slate-600 (7.60) / orange-700 (5.18), pinned by the probe and reverse-checked.
- **The rank bars lost their ghost**: the `from-primary/60` gradient dimming died (the v2.204.0 law — a dimmed mark is a ghost); solid primary carries the real revenue share, and the share denominator hoisted out of the per-row map (an O(n²) re-spread every row).
- **Numeric voices aligned**: the revenue figures and "N sold" chips ride `tabular-nums`; the sold chip grew 10px → 11px.

### Tests
- NEW `src/tests/best-sellers-v2206.test.ts` (45 tests): the fast-moving wire contract (fastMovers naming, the normalizer-hazard ban, the summary strip's real fields), the trends adapter truth (the wire's actual names, the honest share with its zero-total guard, changeKnown, "new" renderings), composeProductsVoice's null-growth branch, and the rank-chip contrast math with the ghosts pinned in reverse.
- The HR reports desk sheds its cascading render: the branch chip's name is now DERIVED from a one-shot stores map — the old per-storeId effect reset the name with a synchronous setState (a cascading render) on every filter clear and re-fetched the stores list per pick. The desk's remaining set-state-in-effect findings are a TOOLCHAIN SPLIT, recorded honestly: the sandbox's drifted plugin 7.1.1 flags them, CI's lockfile-pinned 7.0.1 does not — so no source-level downrules (a disable on CI is an unused-directive ERROR); the drift itself is an ops item for the commit-package-lock policy. Lint: 0 errors on both toolchains (CI 415 warnings on the lockfile toolchain; the sandbox reads 534 on its drifted copy — both under their own baselines).
- Prior suites' pkg pins ride to 2.206.0 (35 suites); the head-pin counters re-anchored 96/97/98/129 → 97/98/99/130; whats-new heads the 2.206.0 entry.
- `verify:v2-206-0` wired — see `scripts/verify-v2-206-0.mjs`. FULL vitest, lint, voice lint, build gates green before push; pre-merge E2E on the accountant persona (Top Products real figures vs the API word-for-word; customer_analysis + rental_performance walked clean — no NaN anywhere).

## [2.205.0] The reports learn to speak, honestly

Two arcs in one round, both born from the worklog's own audit queue — the reports tab (the compliance-adjacent surface an accountant lives in) was the last tab whose charts rode silent pictures AND the only tab still fabricating figures in code. Zero new queries; every fix composes or removes, never invents.

### Fixed — THE HONESTY PASS (fabricated data deleted)
- **The quick-stat change chips died**: all four cards (Today Revenue / Today Sales / Low Stock / Outstanding Debt) rendered HARDCODED change percentages (+12.5% / +8.3% / −5.0% / +3.2%) as if they were recorded data, with arrow indicators. Deleted. In their place each card speaks a REAL companion from the same dashboard wire: the revenue card names the day's loudest payment method with its take, the sales card speaks the real average per sale, the low-stock card adds the out-of-stock count in the -700 voice when it is nonzero, and the debt card carries an honest window note.
- **The fabricated sales comparison died**: the sales report's Total Revenue and Transactions cards computed a "previous period" from `Math.random()` (70–110% of the real total) and rendered the delta as a real % change. The block is gone; the cards speak `avgTransactionValue` and `totalTax` — real wire figures.
- **The hourly chart shows the day's REAL hours**: the "daily/hourly breakdown" was `Math.random()` per render, labeled "mock" only in code. It now maps `hourlySalesBreakdown` off the tab's ALREADY-FETCHED `GET /api/dashboard` wire (live-verified on the seeded day: 10AM Ksh 7,500 / 11AM Ksh 43,000 / 12PM 0), business hours 8AM–6PM, quiet hours kept honestly at zero. The changeIndicator/changeColor helpers died with their only callers, and a source comment warns the next round: a change chip without a real previous period is a fabrication.

### Added — THE ARIA SWEEP (every picture speaks)
- **`src/lib/report-voices.ts`** — the one-writer voice module: seven pure composers (`composeSeriesVoice`, `composeLoudestVoice`, `composeHourlyVoice`, `composeStockHealthVoice`, `composeProductsVoice`, `composeForecastVoice`, `composeNamedTotalsVoice`), all unit-testable without rendering the 2,800-line tab, all taking pre-formatted figures so the tab keeps ONE currency voice (Ksh — the v2.201.1 lesson scoped here). The zero-day law holds: quiet hours say "no sales", a total of nothing never speaks, NaN can never ride into an aria (pinned).
- **The three SVG charts speak** (valuation, hourly, top products — bar AND line variants): `role="img"` with every named point in caller order, its figure and its honest share of the whole; hourly quiet hours speak "no sales"; the loudest hour is named.
- **The five recharts surfaces speak**: the growing/declining product bars (named rows with real growth % + revenue or units-left), the forecast area (predicted points in order + the dashed bounds band named once; the wire carries no method field so none is spoken), the top-customers chart and the rental revenue-by-status chart (semicolon rows with shares + total). The stock-health gauge speaks its verdict with the real healthy/low/out-of-stock counts.

### Style (mandatory)
- **The bars stopped hiding**: SVGBarChart's `opacity-80` dimming died (the v2.204.0 lesson — a dimmed mark is a ghost); hover now brightens instead. The value labels and axis labels grew 8px → 9px, and every numeric label rides `tabular-nums` so the figures align in columns.
- **The bar chart grew a grounding baseline** (a muted non-essential rule) and the line chart's points gained hover-title parity with the bars.
- The quick-stat sub-lines got a consistent 10px muted voice (the out-of-stock warning in red-700, 5.94:1 on white).

### Tests
- NEW `src/tests/reports-honest-voices-v2205.test.ts` (27 tests): the fabrication ban (no Math.random, no salesComparison, no hardcoded chips, no orphaned helpers — CODE-only scan, comments stripped per the v2.204.0 lesson), the real-wire replacements, all eleven aria mounts, the one-currency-voice guard for the composed arias, and the voice module's unit contracts (order, shares, quiet-hour law, empty step-asides, NaN poisoning).
- Prior suites' pkg pins ride to 2.205.0 (the treadmill tax, 107 assertion lines); the head-pin counters re-anchored 95/96/97/128 → 96/97/98/129; whats-new heads the 2.205.0 entry.
- `verify:v2-205-0` wired — see `scripts/verify-v2-205-0.mjs`. FULL vitest, lint, voice lint, build gates green before push; pre-merge E2E on the accountant persona (the Reports tab is role-gated — recorded in the worklog).

## [2.204.0] The pictures learn to speak

The graphics-bar audit. The v2.203.0 round caught ONE sub-3:1 line (the forecast dash at 2.15:1) and this round measured the WHOLE app's meaning-bearing graphics against the same WCAG 2.x non-text 3:1 bar — and found the -500 family failing everywhere it painted a mark on the white card ground. Worse, two places painted TEXT with a GRAPHIC color (the health score numbers, the analytics donut's percentage cells) — text needs 4.5:1, so no -600 mark voice could ever be enough there. And five chart species never spoke their data at all. Zero new queries; every voice composes what the wire already carries.

### Added
- **The Sparkline speaks** (`src/components/org/primitives.tsx`): the 7-day spark was `aria-hidden` decoration while its seven per-day values were spoken NOWHERE — the faces show today's figure and the week total, but the SHAPE (which day was loud, which was quiet) existed only for eyes. A caller that passes `spokenLabel` gets an honest picture: `role="img"` with every value read out in day order; a caller that omits it keeps the old silent decoration. NINE call sites gained voices: the org KPI row's face sparks (gross revenue, settled transactions), the drill-down modal's three lens sparks (gross / net / VAT), the week-by-branch strip (each branch named in its own voice), and the store matrix's cell + workspace-dialog sparks.
- **The Kenya map speaks its ranks**: the dots' `<title>` only answered a hover, so the whole picture was silence for a screen reader. The aria now names every branch loudest-first with its take; the quiet branches are never dropped (a fully quiet org says "every branch quiet so far" in words instead of ranking nothing).
- **The payment donut speaks its split** (branch dashboard): the aria composes "Payment methods today, loudest first: M-Pesa 85.2% (Ksh …) — Ksh … collected" instead of the generic "distribution" (rows arrive pre-sorted by amount). The empty card keeps the old generic voice — no split, nothing to speak.
- **The debt aging bar speaks its buckets**: the face bar composes the four buckets with their amounts and the total; the modal's bar has spoken since v2.201.0 and now rides the same re-voiced family, dots included.
- **The week chart and hourly heatmap speak**: the week chart's aria carries the seven named days in calendar order plus the forecast line's position; the heatmap names the peak hour and its take.
- **The reports pie speaks its split**: the inventory category pie composes its shares loudest-first.

### Fixed
- **THE ROUND'S STYLE — the -500 graphics family re-voiced to -600, measured before ship** (`scripts/probe-graphics-v2204.mjs`, 102/102): every meaning-bearing mark on the white card ground now clears the 3:1 non-text bar. The branch dashboard's donut segments (emerald-500 2.54 → emerald-600 3.77; amber-500 2.15 → amber-600 3.19; gift card slate-400 2.86 → violet-600 5.70, also un-colliding it from Split's slate-500), aging segments (orange-500 2.80 → orange-600 3.56), the org Sparkline's default stroke (2.54 → 3.77), the Kenya map's dots on the slate-100 land fill (darkorange 2.33 / emerald 2.36 / slate-400 2.60 → 3.25 / 3.44 / 4.34), the health gauges (arc + breakdown bars), the analytics KPI accents (sky-500 2.77 → sky-600 4.11; emerald/amber/orange same move), the area chart's line + gradient + cursor + the prev-period dash (slate-400 → slate-500 4.76), the top-products category palette (teal 2.49 / cyan 2.43 / lime 1.98 / yellow 1.92 → the -600/-700 family), the financial chart configs (incl. the debt-aging green-500 2.28 / yellow-500 1.92), the reports TREND_COLORS + category arrays + the forecast bounds (which lost their opacity dimming — a dimmed -600 is still a sub-3:1 ghost), and the security severity/event fills. The passing marks are recorded and stay: DEBT rose-500 3.67, SPLIT slate-500 4.76, CARD blue-500 3.68, DEBT red-500 3.76, SPLIT violet-500 4.23, pink-500 3.52, purple-500 3.96.
- **THE TEXT VOICES SPLIT FROM THE MARKS**: a -600 mark voice is still a failing TEXT voice (emerald-600 text measures 3.77:1 against the 4.5:1 bar) — so wherever a graphic color also painted text, the two voices now live separately: `healthTextColor` (the branch health breakdown's score numbers → the -700 family, 5.02–6.47), the org `HealthGauge`'s animated number (was the stroke color at 2.54:1 whenever the news was good), and the analytics donut's percentage cells (`PAYMENT_METHOD_TEXT`, 5.02–7.08). The security score gauge splits arc (green/yellow -600) from text (green/yellow/red-700 classes).
- The ghosts are pinned BELOW the bar in reverse (`scripts/probe-graphics-v2204.mjs` scene 6: twelve -500 voices + the emerald-600-as-text lesson), and the audited files are scanned CODE-only (comments stripped — a historical note naming an old voice never trips the scan).

### Tests
- NEW `src/tests/graphics-speak-v2204.test.ts` (41 tests): the Sparkline voice (primitive contract, all nine call sites, the unlabeled spark keeping the old aria-hidden contract), the Kenya map's ranks, the donut/aging/week/heatmap/pie voices, the graphic-vs-text voice split across all three gauge species, the analytics + financial + reports + security re-voices, the no-shared-voice rule (no two payment methods wear the same color), the audit enforcement (no audited file paints a mark with the -500 ghost family), the one-currency-voice guard (the org console's spark labels speak KES — its own vocabulary — never Ksh), and the release surfaces.
- **THE PRE-MERGE E2E EARNED ITS KEEP AGAIN**: the live check of the console's composed arias caught the spark labels saying "Ksh" inside the org console's KES voice (the v2.201.1 bug class, new instance) — fixed before the push, the labels re-voiced to KES and the guard test pinned. The QA-gap stays closed.
- Deliberate pin evolutions with reasoning: eight Sparkline source pins across four suites updated for the spokenLabel prop (vat-spark-v2194 ×3, transactions-lens-v2195 ×2, store-matrix-week-v2179 ×2, expert-week-v2176 ×2); prior suites' pkg pins ride to 2.204.0 (the treadmill tax); the head-pin counters re-anchored 94/95/96/127 → 95/96/97/128.
- `verify:v2-204-0` wired — see `scripts/verify-v2-204-0.mjs`. FULL vitest, lint, probe, build gates green before push.

## [2.203.0] The week speaks

The door arc's final quiet surface on the branch dashboard that carries unspoken wire figures: the Sales trend · 7 days card drew the week's shape as a picture — an `role="img"` SVG whose generic aria-label named the chart but whose seven per-day revenues were spoken NOWHERE as text — while `revenueTrend7d` carried every figure the week could speak: `days[]` (date, label, revenue, isToday), `forecast`, `forecastMethod`, `todayIsOutlier`. This release makes the card the branch page's EMERALD door and opens **the branch's week** — zero new queries. (Recorded honestly: PaymentMethodsCard stays deliberately door-less — its every figure — labels, counts, shares, amounts, the total — is already spoken on its face, and a door would duplicate the day ledger's tender-mix voice.)

### Added
- **The branch's week** (`src/components/dashboard/branch-doors.tsx`): `WeekLedgerModal` + the `BranchWeekWire` structural subset — the headline speaking the named week's total and day count (`branch-week-total`/`branch-week-meta`), the loudest day in words (`branch-week-loudest`), the seven named days IN CALENDAR ORDER (a week is a timeline, not a ranking — the rows speak in the order the days happened; the loudest earns a line instead of a reorder) each with its revenue and honest `% of the week` (`branch-week-row`/`branch-week-share`), the today chip on the wire's own `isToday` flag (`branch-week-today-chip`), the **zero days speaking "no sales"** instead of a percentage of nothing (`branch-week-zero`), the forecast block with the wire's method verbatim and today's honest progress — "Today so far Ksh X · NN% of the forecast" only when both figures are real (`branch-week-forecast`/`branch-week-delta`), the wire's own outlier voice (`branch-week-outlier`), the empty step-aside for a week with no named days yet, and the honest window note (`branch-week-window`). The dark half from birth; no anchors; null rows filtered by predicate; the zero-total rule (a share of a zero week is never spoken).
- **The card face joins the door arc** (`dashboard-tab.tsx`): the Sales trend card wears the emerald door vocabulary — `branch-week-card` as a role=button with the "- open details" aria, keyboard open, the emerald edge bar + live dot lit only when the week has data, the family focus ring — and its description now says what it opens.
- **Today's progress on the face** (`branch-week-progress`): an emerald "Today so far" row against the forecast with a capped bar (the TEXT speaks the real percentage even past 100; the BAR caps at full — the day is not over, so the voice is a progress read, never an UP/DOWN verdict).

### Fixed
- **THE ROUND'S STYLE — a real graphics-contrast catch, measured before ship**: the trend chart's FORECAST DASHED LINE rode amber-500 on white — **2.15:1, below the 3:1 non-text graphics bar** (the legend text carried the figure, but the line is the graphic that carries the forecast's position). The line and the legend swatch re-voice to **amber-600 (3.19:1)** and the ghost value is pinned BELOW the bar in REVERSE (`scripts/probe-week-doors-v2203.mjs` 16/16 pre-derivation; `scripts/contrast-math-week-doors-v2203.mjs` **18/18**). Both label rows grew from 9px to 10px for readability — the trend card's day labels (today's label became a real chip: emerald-50/emerald-700 ring-emerald-200, dark variants measured) and the hourly chart's hour labels — and the forecast figure rides tabular-nums. The tab's remaining 9px voices (axis ticks, badges, the donut's one-word caption) are pre-existing chip/numeric contexts, recorded and left.

### Tests
- NEW `src/tests/branch-week-door-v2203.test.ts`: the wire purity (structural subset + compile-time assignability guard against field drift, the v2.202.1 lesson); the calendar-order law (no reorder); the honest shares + zero-total rule; the zero-day "no sales" voice; the loudest line; the forecast block (method verbatim, the delta only when both figures are real); the outlier voice; the empty step-aside; the window note; the no-anchors law; the door face pins (testId, aria, edge bar, live dot only with data, hook order); the amber-600 re-voice + the ghost pinned in reverse; the release surfaces.
- Deliberate pin evolutions with reasoning: prior suites' pkg pins ride to 2.203.0 (the treadmill tax); head-pin counters re-anchored (93/94/95/126 → 94/95/96/127).
- `verify:v2-203-0` wired — see `scripts/verify-v2-203-0.mjs`. FULL vitest, lint, build gates green before push.

## [2.202.1] The branch slice fixed

### Fixed
- The org desk's six branch slices rendered their empty step-aside ("The summary names no branches yet") on the LIVE console despite five branches on the wire: the doors' structural subset read `storeId` where `GET /api/org/summary`'s rows carry `WorkspaceStoreRef.id`, so the predicate filtered every branch out. The subset now reads `id` (`desk-doors.tsx`), and the class kill: `org-console.tsx` pins `OrgSummary extends OrgDeskWire` at COMPILE time — a field-name drift between the subset and the real wire can no longer compile. Found by the round's agent-browser E2E on the live console (the second release in a row where the post-merge E2E earned its keep).

## [2.202.0] The org's quiet row speaks

The door arc walked three surfaces — the /console KPI command row (v2.195-v2.199, six voices), the branch workspaces' KPI row (v2.200.0, three) and bottom grid (v2.201.0, two) — while the ORG CONSOLE's "Combined organization metrics" section still rendered its SIX tiles as silent divs, even though the summary wire (`GET /api/org/summary`) has carried the per-branch slices all along: `stores[]` with each branch's todayRevenue, todayTransactions, lowStockCount, overdueRentals, debtOutstanding + debtCount and pendingMpesa. This release gives the six tiles the door vocabulary and ONE parametrized branch-slice modal — zero new queries.

### Added
- **The six doors** (`org-console.tsx` + `desk-doors.tsx`): `StatCard` gains the door vocabulary (`onDoor` / `doorTestId` / `doorEdgeClass` / `doorDotClass` / `doorHot` — role=button, keyboard, the "- open details" aria voice, the family edge bar, the live dot, the hot ring lit ONLY when the metric's org total is nonzero). The six tiles wear the /console KPI row's own face inventory (orange revenue, blue transactions, amber stock, rose rentals, red debt, indigo pending) and open **the branch slice** for each (`org-desk-card-{metric}`).
- **The branch slice** (`src/components/org/desk-doors.tsx`): ONE parametrized modal (`BranchSliceModal` + the `ORG_DESK_METRICS` registry — the one-writer rule) speaking the named branch rows loudest-first (`org-desk-row-{metric}`) with each branch's honest `% of the org`, the count sub-line where the wire carries one (transactions ride the revenue rows; the debt book's bill count rides the debt rows), the **quiet branches speaking their 0** (`org-desk-quiet-{metric}` — never dropped), the honest headline (`org-desk-total-{metric}` — the tile's figure stays the truth), the zero-day clear line, the empty step-aside, and the **window note** — the slice reads the branches named on this summary, the branches table carries each workspace's door (the pointer in words, not a link — the honest-door law). The zero-total rule: no percentage of nothing.
- **The voice lint** (`scripts/voice-lint.mjs`): the v2.201.1 lesson made mechanical — a grep for the double-currency class (a literal "KES"/"Ksh" wrapped around formatKES, which already speaks "Ksh") across the UI sources; zero findings on the current tree, a finding is a release blocker.
- **The house laws hold from birth**: the panel paints its dark half (the org desk's mount of the dark-panel law); no anchors; null wire rows filtered by predicate; the hook-order law; the RBAC is the summary's own (the doors compose the slices the viewer already receives).

### Fixed
- **THE ROUND'S STYLE — the six-hue vocabulary measured BEFORE ship** (`scripts/probe-desk-doors-v2202.mjs`, 36/36): the -700 accents 5.05-8.07:1 on white, the -50 chip grounds 4.87-7.22:1, the -300 dark accents 8.88-12.34:1 on slate-900, the -500/10 dark chip stacks 8.05-10.63:1, the edges as graphics 3.19-6.44:1 over the white card. ONE honest alignment: the branches table's overdue cell rode `text-red-600` on bg-card (4.76:1 — a PASS on the installed palette) and re-voiced to `red-700` (6.42:1) as the Task-188 family alignment, recorded as alignment, not violation (`scripts/contrast-math-desk-doors-v2202.mjs`, 42/42).

### Tests
- NEW `src/tests/desk-doors-v2202.test.ts` (30 tests): the wire purity (no fetch/db, structural subset, the predicate-filter law); the six-metric registry in the face hues; the ONE modal one-writer rule; the loudest-first sort; the honest shares + zero-total rule; the quiet-branch voice; the count sub-lines; the clear/empty voices; the no-anchors law; the six card handles + hook-order law + honest hot ring + one-writer wiring; the HR tiles keeping their hrefs; the family voices; the dark-panel law; the alignment record; the probe + voice lint pins; the release surfaces.
- Deliberate pin evolutions with reasoning: prior suites' pkg pins ride to 2.202.0 (the treadmill tax); head-pin counters re-anchored (91/92/93/124 → 92/93/94/125).

## [2.201.1] A cleaner debt book voice

### Fixed
- The debt book and the day's movers spoke one word twice: the risk lines and the movers' meta prefixed "KES" in front of `formatKES`'s own "Ksh" — "KES Ksh 88,400.00" on every named debtor's lifetime-spend line and the movers' headline total. The double prefix is gone; the figures read "Ksh 88,400.00" — same numbers, one currency voice (`branch-doors.tsx`, four template sites). Found by the round's own agent-browser E2E on the seeded Juja Main ledger (the QA step ran against the shipped tag; the fix rides an honest patch, not a rewritten one).

## [2.201.0] The debt book and the movers speak

The branch KPI row found its three voices in v2.200.0 — and the assessment that picked this round walked the door arc down the page's bottom grid, where TWO cards still rendered their stories in silence even though every figure they could drill into already rode the dashboard wire: the Debt Aging SUMMARY card spoke total + buckets + a bill count while `debtCrisis.customers` carried the wire's top-10 NAMED debtors (name, owes, lifetimeSpend, ratio, highRisk) and `highRiskCount` / `warning` / `banner` carried the alarm; the Top Selling Products card ranked today's movers five deep while the wire names TEN and a category mix (`topSellingCategories`, the route's full GROUP BY over the day's lines) the tab had never read. Both cards become doors; both modals compose the SAME answer the cards show — zero new queries.

### Added
- **The branch's debt book** (`branch-doors.tsx` + `dashboard-tab.tsx`): the Debt Aging card becomes the debt family's ROSE door (`testId="branch-debt-card"`, edge bar `bg-rose-600`, the live dot and the hot ring lit ONLY by the wire's own `warning` flag) and opens **The branch's debt book** — the aging bar at reading size (`branch-debt-bar`, role="img" naming all four buckets and the total), the four bucket cells (`branch-debt-bucket-current/d30/d60/d90plus`, each with dot + label + amount + an honest `% of the book`; a zero bucket draws nothing and says "none"), the named debtors loudest-first (`branch-debt-row`, re-sorted client-side) with each debtor's share of the book and the **wire's own risk voice** (a high-risk debtor owes more than twice their lifetime spend — the server's rule spoken in words, never re-derived; the wire's ratio speaks as "% of it owed"; a debtor with no completed purchases says so), the high-risk count line (`branch-debt-hot`), the server's warning banner verbatim (`branch-debt-banner`), the unnamed step-aside, the clear-book line (`branch-debt-clear`), and the **honest window note** — the book names its loudest debtors, the BILL count stays the truth (`branch-debt-window`).
- **The day's movers** (`branch-doors.tsx`): the Top Products card becomes the VIOLET door — a new hue for products (`testId="branch-movers-card"`, the hot ring lit only when the day has movers) and opens **The day's movers** — the headline with the named window's totals and the loudest mover spoken in words (`branch-movers-top`), the FULL named roster (the wire's 10, not the card's 5; `branch-movers-row`) with qty, revenue and an honest `% of the named window`, and the **category mix** (`branch-movers-cat-row`) with the wire's own category colors and honest `% of the mix` (silent when its named total is zero — no percentage of nothing); the window note names what the doors read (`branch-movers-window`); the zero day speaks its line (`branch-movers-clear`). The tab's type gains `topSellingCategories` as a structural subset the wire already carries.
- **The house laws hold from birth**: both panels paint their dark half (`dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900` — the branch page's next TWO mounts of the dark-panel law, one shared chrome constant); the doors are self-contained focus views with **no anchors** (the honest-door law); null wire rows are filtered by type predicate, never a non-null assertion; the hook-order law (door state before any early return); the doors render only inside the FULL dashboard (the limited row untouched).

### Fixed
- **THE ROUND'S SWEEP — the -600 straggler family closed on the branch dashboard** (measured by the pre-derivation probe `scripts/probe-quiet-doors-v2201.mjs` BEFORE anything was re-voiced): the Alerts count badge white-on-`bg-red-500` **3.82:1** → `bg-red-700` **6.42:1**; the payment DEBT chip `rose-600` on `rose-100` **3.75:1** → `rose-700` **5.04:1**; the GIFT_CARD chip `slate-500` on `slate-100` **4.35:1** → `slate-600` **6.90:1**; the alert CRITICAL chip `red-600` on `red-100` **3.90:1** → `red-700` **5.26:1**; the crisis banner sub-line and the trend DOWN chip `red-600` on `red-50` **4.36:1** → `red-700` **5.88:1** (the v2.199 login-error pairing, third instance). RECORDED HONESTLY as alignment, not violation: `red-600` on white PASSES the installed palette at 4.76:1, so the KPI card values, the shift drawer's cash-out, the top-customers' "Owes", the high-risk avatar initials and the debt card's own total moved `-600` → `-700` as FAMILY ALIGNMENT (the v2.199 Store-Matrix precedent) plus margin; the debt book's own figures rode in at a knife-edge 4.51:1 and were moved to `rose-700`/`rose-300` (6.06/9.29). The five draft pairs are pinned below the bar in REVERSE.
- **The v2.200.0 round's carried nit**: the receipts-by-hour aria spoke "with 1 receipts" — now "1 receipt" / "N receipts" (`kpi-doors.tsx`).

### Tests
